# Tuya OTA firmware catalogue: channel 0

This collection contains firmware reported for Tuya channel 0 (Main Module). The channel identifies the component, not its chipset.

951 unique cached payloads are listed below. Tuya JSON release notes are available for 838 offering rows; 128 captured responses supplied no description. 216 historical URL-list rows have no captured OTA response. Blank changelog cells are intentional; no release notes are inferred from filenames or product descriptions.

The product key and version belong to the individual offering. A payload may serve multiple products; identical bytes are stored once. Original download filenames and hash mappings are retained in the private catalogue. Channel-0 files retain their original Tuya download filenames. Device/model and platform/MCU information is retained in separate columns.

Files over 100 MiB are local-only and deliberately have no repository-file link. Unavailable downloads remain listed without a local-file link. [All channels](../README.md).
